ODIS regional buses and Opava town buses and trolleybuses · Opavsko · Vítkovsko · Hlučínsko · MHD Opava · vypravenost · Tuesday 20 Oct 2026

ODIS Opavsko Vítkovsko Hlučínsko

The ODIS bus lines of three regional contract areas (Opavsko and Vítkovsko of Z-Group bus, Hlučínsko of Transdev Slezsko) and the Opava town network of Městský dopravní podnik Opava, buses and trolleybuses, set as Simutrans lines with the owner's regional method: each line read over the whole day at its core points, a repeating interval, destination and shift variants on their own longer intervals (at most 480 min), timetables at the terminals worked back from the knots, every afternoon stop on a line, buses sized from the pattern. Buses are the ones seen on each line on the ODIS live map on Tuesday 20 Oct 2026.

Network state, 20 Oct 2026

What is in the model and what is left out, with the reason.

modelledThree regional contract areas and the Opava town network

Opavsko (licences 902xxx) and Vítkovsko (903xxx) of Z-Group bus, Hlučínsko (904xxx) of Transdev Slezsko, and the town lines of Opava (905xxx) of Městský dopravní podnik Opava. Each has its own section below; the trolleybuses are a section of their own.

modelledOpava trolleybuses 201–204

Their timetables are in CIS JŘ's city-transport JDF (traction T), not in the bus export; they are read the same way and modelled as trolleybus lines (the owner asked for them).

modelledOverhead wire

Each trolleybus route (OpenStreetMap route relations) was checked against the wire mapped in OpenStreetMap, every 10 m, 25 m tolerance. 201, 202 and 203 are under the wire all the way. 204 has two unwired stretches: Ratibořská – Vrchní – U Cukrovaru – Pekařská – Divadlo (about 2.0 km without wire) and Kylešovice, Bílovecká – Hlavní – škola (0.6 km in a straight line between the stops, about 1 km by the timetable; the OSM relation stops at Bílovecká, so this end was added from the timetable's stops, which have no wire within 100 m). Wikipedia marks the same stops as partial-trolleybus stops. 202's single daily trip to Malé Hoštice also leaves the wire; it is not modelled.

left outTrolleybus 204: Vrchní street closure (12–23 Oct)

Road repairs on Vrchní street in Kateřinky: one-way 12–20 Oct, full closure 21–23 Oct. On the vehicle day 204 ran with replacement buses via Partyzánská (trips 40xx on the live map, MDPO Citelis 150–157). The closure ends this week, so 204 is modelled as the regular trolleybus line with designed trolleybuses (owner check).

left outShort diversions: the regular routes are modelled

241 and 243 (Opava, Psychiatrická léčebna not served, 28 Sep – 23 Nov), 262 (the same stop, 5 Oct – 23 Nov), 253 (Služovice, obecní úřad, 7 Sep – 31 Oct) and 284 (Dolní Benešov, Zábřeh, Mexiko, 16–21 Oct; also 256 and 275, which are listed). Short works are modelled as the regular state.

modelledDiversions to the end of the timetable year

234, 242 and 261 skip Březová, střed until 11 Dec; 263 and 264 run diversion versions with the same stops until 12 Dec. They are modelled as they run.

left outLines shared with other sheets

615, 620, 691, 692 and 693 (minor Vítkovsko/Opavsko licences) are on the Novojičínsko/Bílovecko sheet. This sheet models 260 and 920 (also Bruntálsko/Rýmařovsko licences), 261, 263, 270, 285 and 678 (also Novojičínsko/Bílovecko licences) and 555 (the Havířovsko/Karvinsko/Orlovsko sheet lists it as Hlučínsko's).

Each bus shows its fleet number and branch, full type and length (with its number plate), and pak128 core with livery. Green border: seen running this line on the live map that afternoon (the line's window first; the trip is named). Dashed border: designed, not seen in the window, with the reason. Dimmed, dotted: seen, not needed on this line.

How to read the line cards

Typing the timetable. Values go into the Scheduling tab of the terminal entry, with the calendar on: Departure every, Offset, Also at (at most 7). Offsets count from 01:00 and every terminal is set as a loop, so each value is (real departure − 61) mod interval; the real clock minute is under every value. A row with Every 0 has no timetable: the bus leaves on arrival.

Core points and knots. Each line was read over the whole operating day (5:00–23:00) at its core points: terminals, knots where variants split, shared hubs. A trip counts at the time it passes a core point, wherever it starts. Where a slot is defined at a knot, the terminal departure is the knot time minus the running time, and the knot gets no timetable.

Variants. Each destination, path or shift variant is its own Simutrans line on a longer interval (the save game's Ex4). Shift lines repeat every 480 (8 h), the longest interval allowed. Trips more than 3 min off every slot of the line are off the pattern and shown in orange.

Buses needed. One bus followed round the line's own slots with the real running times and at least 3 min at each terminal (less where the slots chain closer, as the real timetable turns). Real buses swap lines at Opava, Vítkov and Hlučín all afternoon, so per-line totals are higher than what Transdev runs; pooling lines would be the owner's choice and is not done.

To check

First, seven choices made here that you may want to flip, each with its alternative. Then the Simutrans lines whose model window has a slot without a real departure, or a real departure more than 3 min off: mostly the real timetable is irregular there, and the card says why.

How this was built

Scope. Licences with the area prefixes 902 (Opavsko) and 903 (Vítkovsko) of Z-Group bus, 904 (Hlučínsko) of Transdev Slezsko and 905 (MHD Opava) of Městský dopravní podnik Opava; the trolleybus lines 201–204 (905201–905204) from CIS JŘ's city-transport JDF (draha/mestske, downloaded 21 Oct 2026), the rest in the CIS JŘ bus export (CIS JŘ bus JDF export of 20 Oct 2026 (stored by the Havířov/Karviná/Orlová pipeline)), evaluated for Tuesday 20 Oct 2026 with the shared version rule. The prefixes match the live map (Z-Group, pr. Opava; Transdev Slezsko pr. Ostrava; MDPO) and the fleet numbers (22-, 23-, 24-). A line holding a second licence on the Bruntálsko or Novojičínsko sheet is modelled on the sheet with most of its trips. 70 ODIS lines; 59 are modelled here, 5 are on other sheets, the rest are listed with the reason.

Method. pipeline/bus-schedule-method.md (v3, the owner's regional rules of 20 Oct 2026): whole-day reading at core points, repeating interval, Ex4 variants, timing at terminals worked back from knots, no daily lines (at most every 480), every afternoon stop on a line, buses from the pattern, every line checked by hand. Each line's choices are in config.json → lines and in the README.

Buses. The ODIS live map (mpvnet.cz) polled every minute 13:05–19:30 on Tuesday 20 Oct 2026 by the Havířov/Karviná/Orlová pipeline gives every bus's trips (licence and trip number), joined to fleet numbers by number plate (seznam-autobusu.cz register). A bus fills one Simutrans line it ran in that line's window, by matching. Buses already on another sheet (Ostrava Bus Vypravenost, section All lines at Hranečník; Havířov Karviná Orlová Bus Vypravenost (town lines); ODIS Regional Havířovsko Karvinsko Orlovsko; ODIS Těšínsko Třinecko Frýdecko; ODIS Novojičínsko Bílovecko) are not used here. MDPO's vehicles appear on the live map as MDPO<fleet number> and are joined to the MDPO register by number. Trolleybus 204 ran with replacement buses on the day (Vrchní street closure, 12–23 Oct); it is modelled as the regular trolleybus line, and its trolleybuses are designed: MDPO trolleybuses not in service that afternoon, with an auxiliary drive for its unwired sections. A trolleybus with an auxiliary drive shows it after its type (+ battery, + diesel generator), from its register page.

Sources: CIS JŘ bus JDF, ODIS live map, Transdev Slezsko register, CIS JŘ city-transport JDF (Opava trolleybuses), Z-Group bus, MDPO and Transdev Morava registers, MDPO notice on the Vrchní closure, overhead wire and trolleybus routes from OpenStreetMap (Overpass, 22 Oct 2026), checked against Wikipedia’s partial-trolleybus stops; battery and diesel-generator trolleybuses from their register pages (“alternativní pohon”).
